# Contract function reference

Generated from the source-bound Solidity ABI and reviewed explanations. Update the notes and regenerate; do not edit this file by hand.

Covers **39 concrete project contracts, 906 functions, 39 constructors and 13 receive hooks**. Inherited ERC-721 and governance functions are included. Internal-only libraries, abstract bases and external dependency interfaces are not separate operator targets.

**Reads** use `view`/`pure`; on-chain calls still consume gas and can revert. **Writes** require a transaction and the stated permissions. A returned value from a write is available to simulation or another contract; a wallet receipt does not automatically contain that return value. Functions with no ABI outputs change state and/or emit events; no artificial return is invented.

`msg.sender`, payable `msg.value`, network/contract identity and operating-state guards are not ABI arguments. Payable does not mean arbitrary ETH is accepted. Unnamed parameters retain explicit zero-based positions. Tuple fields are expanded in ABI order; integer units and zero-address meaning depend on the reviewed operation, not the Solidity type alone.

The reference documents an undeployed candidate. It is not permission to broadcast, select providers or approve production. **Production NOT APPROVED.**

Missing function-purpose, caller or payable-value explanations: **0**. Top-level input positions without a dedicated explanation: **0**. Output positions without a dedicated explanation: **0**; their result meaning may instead be explained by the function purpose. These counts do not claim that every nested tuple field has a separate prose annotation. Missing semantics are not guessed from a Solidity type or vague name.

## ZZZAccessController

Direct governance-wallet authorization, fixed module binding/sealing, independent keeper/guardian roles and canonical lifecycle. No timelock, role scheduler or generic transaction executor.

[Source](../src/ZZZAccessController.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Select governance, keeper and guardian; deploy fixed helper factories. Deployer holds temporary bootstrap only until seal. Emits the initial KeeperChanged and GuardianChanged grants. | Deployment only; three static nonzero address arguments. A governance wallet cannot be the controller itself. | `initialGovernance: address` — Reviewed governance wallet, typically a multisig; after sealing it calls configuration targets directly. Its own signatures/nonce authorize transactions.<br>`initialKeeper: address` — Nonzero initial bounded-maintenance operator; role changes separately from governance.<br>`initialGuardian: address` — Nonzero initial pause-only guardian; governance handover does not automatically rotate this role. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZActivationAssets

Fixed ETH/ZZZ-only contribution-source policy. ZZZ identity is selected once before sealing; same-token admission/pins remain governed. Pair accounting and NFT marketplace royalty currencies are separate.

[Source](../src/ZZZActivationAssets.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Bind the immutable controller and Registry through the controller's fixed factory, without an arbitrary direct-construction bypass. | Only controller.activationAssetsFactory(); that factory's access() must equal controller. The fixed factory derives poolRegistry from its own create() caller. | `controller: address` — Existing AccessController contract; must be the factory's immutable controller.<br>`poolRegistry: address` — Nonzero Registry/caller address bound to this unique child by the factory. | None in ABI. |

| `SOURCE_POLICY_VERSION()`<br>view | Return the explicit fixed ETH/ZZZ input-policy profile version. | Read-only; any caller. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Version1: native ETH plus the one pre-seal ZZZ identity, with no third user contribution currency. |
| `access()`<br>view | Read the immutable controller binding. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract IZZZAccess) — Controller address; not a treasury or royalty payout address. |
| `activePolicy(address)`<br>view | Read the current source-admission policy ID for an asset. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: address` — Actual asset address; zero means native ETH. | `output0: bytes32` — Current append-only source-policy ID; zero means unset, not a healthy enabled source. |
| `isContributionAsset(address)`<br>view | Check permitted source identity only: native ETH or the configured ZZZ. Does not itself check enablement, runtime, price or precision. | Read-only; any caller. | `token: address` — Candidate original user-supplied currency; zero means native ETH. | `output0: bool` — True for native ETH or the fixed nonzero ZZZ identity, even when that identity's current source policy is disabled. value(nonzero) enforces the additional live policy checks. |
| `policies(bytes32)`<br>view | Read immutable source policy token, enabled state, code/review pins, decimals and admission provenance. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: bytes32` — Immutable source-asset admission policy ID, obtained from activePolicy(asset) or policyAt(ordinal). | `token: address` — Actual token identity; zero denotes native ETH for an existing native policy.<br>`enabled: bool` — Whether this recorded source policy enables contributions; existence/identity/prices still require validation.<br>`expectedCodeHash: bytes32` — Expected token runtime hash, or the explicit native identity marker; not proxy implementation attestation.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Recorded governance/admission review commitment; not proof of independent review.<br>`decimals: uint8` — Admitted raw token precision,0..18; zero is also an unset mapping default and must not imply a valid policy.<br>`poolAdmission: bool` — True if this record originated through initial authenticated pool-asset admission, rather than an explicit source-policy review. |

| `policyCount()`<br>view | Read the total immutable source-policy history length. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Number of append-only recorded source policies, including superseded/disabled records. |

| `admitPoolAsset(address)`<br>nonpayable | Initially record a target-pair currency without widening contribution inputs. | `setAsset(address,bool,bytes32,bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Append and activate same-identity source enablement/runtime policy. Cannot introduce a third input currency or change the fixed ZZZ address. Disabling deliberately avoids failed token and price reads. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. | `token: address` — Native ETH zero or fixed ZZZ when enabling; another historical token may only be disabled, not admitted as a third source.<br>`enabled: bool` — True validates and enables the permitted source identity; false stops future nonzero contributions without changing existing LP or claims.<br>`expectedCodeHash: bytes32` — Current reviewed token runtime hash, or NATIVE_ASSET_CODE_HASH for ETH. Disable paths record the supplied pin without querying a failing dependency.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero commitment documenting this source-policy change. | `id: bytes32` — New immutable policy ID; prior history is preserved. |

Both raw floors may explicitly be zero; positive floors still bind. Minima are raw close proceeds; native token0 uses wei. reviewHash must be nonzero. | None in ABI. |
| `validateZZZ(address,bytes32,bytes32)`<br>view | Check the existing bootstrap-only fixed ZZZ/exit/buyback identity before immutable commitment.<br>Requires unsealed and unset parent currentZZZ; source Assets.zzz and Buyback.zzz must match. Parent retains governance pre/post, writes and event. No token retargeting or new recipient. | Only immutable creating Migration; read-only. | `token: address` — Intended fixed deployment ZZZ address; must have code and be admitted to Rewards.<br>`codeHash: bytes32` — Exact current token runtime code hash.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero reviewed fixed-token commitment. | None in ABI. |
| `verifyConsolidation((uint256,bytes32,uint8,int24,int24,bytes32,bool,uint128,uint128,uint128,uint256,bytes32),bool,(bytes32,uint128,uint256,uint256))`<br>view | Verify the actual same-main result and mandatory unchanged controls after reinvestment.<br>Requires same main immutable/accounting/control proof, mainL&gt;=oldL+positive caller delta, zero auxiliary and exact global-count decrement. Guarded main remains active; proof does not rely on USD when force bypasses it. | Only immutable creating Migration; read-only. | `r: (uint256,bytes32,uint8,int24,int24,bytes32,bool,uint128,uint128,uint128,uint256,bytes32)` (struct EmergencyRebalance) — EmergencyRebalance in exact ABI order: bucket(shared custody account); custodyHash(current Migration.consolidationCustodyHash after pause, including recovery nonce/epoch and policy controls); checks(guarded14; forced0..31 retained bits1 raw trigger/cooldown,2 independent price agreement,4 value/quality/idle limits,8 operation flags,16 sampled evidence); lower/upper(EXACT existing main ticks); swapPool/zeroForOne/amountIn/minimumOut(guarded zero/false/0/0 for automatic reviewed routing, forced explicit admitted venue/direction/raw input/positive output or zero no-swap); minimumLiquidity(positive ADDED main liquidity delta, not total L/USD); deadline(now..now+600); reviewHash(nonzero source-bound commitment). No range/destination/custody-recipient change and no compound-reserve release.<br>`r.bucket: uint256`<br>`r.custodyHash: bytes32`<br>`r.checks: uint8`<br>`r.lower: int24`<br>`r.upper: int24`<br>`r.swapPool: bytes32`<br>`r.zeroForOne: bool`<br>`r.amountIn: uint128`<br>`r.minimumOut: uint128`<br>`r.minimumLiquidity: uint128`<br>`r.deadline: uint256`<br>`r.reviewHash: bytes32`<br>`forced: bool` — False requires guarded checks14 and zero explicit-swap fields; true deliberately selects the exact swap/mask. Caller remains current paused governance in both modes.<br>`beforeState: (bytes32,uint128,uint256,uint256)` (struct ZZZCustodyValidation.Consolidation) — Consolidation snapshot in exact ABI order: mainHash(mandatory unchanged pool/main/full-info/owner/centre/clock/shares/deposit/registry/policy/governance/nonce/epoch proof), liquidity(pre-operation uint128 main L), secondary(actual prior residual ID or0), count(prior global registered residual count). Only capture immediately before this same atomic call; never synthesize a proof.<br>`beforeState.mainHash: bytes32`<br>`beforeState.liquidity: uint128`<br>`beforeState.secondary: uint256`<br>`beforeState.count: uint256` | None in ABI. |
| `verifyResidualClose(bytes,(bytes32,uint256,uint256,uint256))`<br>view | Verify unchanged main/current authority controls, residual removal, recovery freshness and exact raw receipt floors.<br>Original Slippage semantics retained. Snapshot has original main L; raw close never increases/replaces it. | Only immutable creating Migration; read-only. | `encoded: bytes` — ABI-encoded ParkingRequest in its documented exact field order, with current main/pool/shares/deposit/tokens/nonce/epoch/portfolio fingerprint, raw residual minimum0/1, deadline and reviewHash. It is not a raw selector or arbitrary transaction.<br>`beforeState: (bytes32,uint256,uint256,uint256)` (struct ZZZCustodyValidation.ResidualClose) — Raw-close snapshot in exact ABI order: mainHash(exact unchanged main/control including L), idle0(previous attributed ETH wei), idle1(previous attributed ZZZ units), secondary(actual nonzero residual ID). Returned by captureResidualClose inside the same atomic parent call.<br>`beforeState.mainHash: bytes32`<br>`beforeState.idle0: uint256`<br>`beforeState.idle1: uint256`<br>`beforeState.secondary: uint256` | `amount0: uint256` — Actual attributed ETH idle increase, excluding old idle/fees.<br>`amount1: uint256` — Actual attributed fixed-ZZZ idle increase, excluding old idle/fees. |

## ZZZFeeController

Allocate physically received local LP fees at 80/10/5/5 into backed holder, collective, compound and buyback ledgers; no creator inbox or remote intake.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy and bind Funded 80/10/5/5 allocation, segregated compound/buyback reserves and optional creator-inbox continuity. | Deployment only; required creator/controller/parent checks apply before this instance can be bound/sealed. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController address for this deployment’s roles and lifecycle. | None in ABI. |

| `access()`<br>view | Identify the fixed controller that supplies this component’s module, governance and lifecycle authorization.<br>Reading the controller address grants no authority. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract IZZZAccess) — The fixed controller that supplies this component’s module, governance and lifecycle authorization. |
| `accounted(address)`<br>view | Read the controller’s total classified backing in one currency.<br>Includes purpose-bound reserves and tracked dust, not an administrator’s spendable balance. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in the native-aware ledger. | `output0: uint256` — The controller’s total classified backing in one currency. |
| `buybackBalance(address)`<br>view | Read the currency amount reserved solely for checked buyback/burn. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in the native-aware ledger. | `output0: uint256` — The currency amount reserved solely for checked buyback/burn. |
| `canonicalBucketRecord(uint256,address)`<br>view | Export the four-word bucket/asset/dust/compound reserve row for the LP-only V3 handoff.<br>Returns: Version-specific ABI bytes, not arbitrary callable data. | Anyone; read-only. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths. | `output0: bytes` — Version-specific ABI bytes, not arbitrary callable data. |
| `canonicalModule()`<br>view | Locate the fixed companion that exports/imports this component’s canonical accounting. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` — The fixed companion that exports/imports this component’s canonical accounting. |
| `canonicalState()`<br>view | Read the controller’s current canonical lifecycle state used by funding/forwarding guards. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint8` — The controller’s current canonical lifecycle state used by funding/forwarding guards. |
| `compoundBalance(uint256,address)`<br>view | Read one bucket/currency amount reserved solely for permanent LP compounding. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID.<br>`input1: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in V2. | `output0: uint256` — One bucket/currency amount reserved solely for permanent LP compounding. |
| `dust(uint256,address)`<br>view | Read one bucket/currency’s unsplit fee remainder awaiting a valid allocation boundary. | Anyone; read-only. | `input0: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT ID.<br>`input1: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in V2. | `output0: uint256` — One bucket/currency’s unsplit fee remainder awaiting a valid allocation boundary. |

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `allocate(uint256,address,uint256)`<br>payable | Receive actual harvested bucket fees and apply the fixed 80/10/5/5 allocation.<br>**Native value:** Exactly amount when token is native zero; zero when token is ERC20 and the exact amount is pulled.<br>Uses raw units; residual sub-batch dust stays tracked. Does not include principal or mint revenue. | Only the fixed Vault in active canonical accounting. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`token: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH only in native-aware paths.<br>`amount: uint256` — Raw units of the actual specified currency; not its USD value. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalBarrier()`<br>nonpayable | Reject a handoff capture from inside an allocation/reserve callback; no balance or entitlement is changed. | Only the fixed FeesCanonical companion, within its lifecycle guards. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalFlush(uint256,address)`<br>nonpayable | Settle captured home-bucket fee dust as part of canonical export preparation. | Only fixed FeesCanonical. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalSend(address,address,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Transfer the exact asset amount during the fixed companion's classified handoff funding/abort procedure; no administrator sweep or arbitrary call. | Only the immutable FeesCanonical companion; its coordinator/lifecycle and exact-backing guards apply. | `asset: address` — Actual admitted reserve currency; address zero means native ETH.<br>`destination: address` — The exact destination chosen by the authenticated canonical funding/abort procedure.<br>`amount: uint256` — Exact raw units assigned to this backing transfer; not an amount fabricated from USD. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalWrite(uint8,bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Import exact LP-only V3 asset or bucket rows under the fixed companion; old receiver/remote codecs are rejected.<br>No arbitrary storage or allocation rewrite. | Only fixed FeesCanonical. | `kind: uint8` — Explicit versioned record/proof type; unsupported values revert.<br>`record: bytes` — Exact versioned ABI-encoded canonical record authenticated against its source. | None in ABI. |
| `flushDust(uint256,address)`<br>nonpayable | Assign tracked sub-20-unit fee dust before a relevant membership/share boundary.<br>Preserves old entitlements and backing; not a free dust sweep. | Only sealed Vault or Membership. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`token: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH only in native-aware paths. | None in ABI. |
| `releaseBuyback(address,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Release only the classified buyback reserve to fixed Buyback for checked execution.<br>Rejects zero/excess amount; does not let a keeper redirect revenue. | Only fixed Buyback. | `token: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH only in native-aware paths.<br>`amount: uint256` — Raw units of the actual specified currency; not its USD value. | None in ABI. |
| `releaseCompound(uint256,address)`<br>nonpayable | Send a bucket’s designated compounding reserve back to Vault for reinvestment.<br>Cannot spend buyback or claimable balances. Returns: Raw reserve amount released; may be zero. | Only fixed authorized Vault. | `id: uint256` — Canonical shared bucket ID, not an individual NFT or physical LP NFT ID.<br>`token: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH only in native-aware paths. | `amount: uint256` — Raw reserve amount released; may be zero. |

| `canonicalBacking(uint256)`<br>view | Read one captured backing asset and raw amount for fee reserves, dust, allocation counters and optional receiver protection.<br>Recorded liability/principal backing, not the module’s unclassified live balance. | Anyone; read-only. | `index: uint256` — Zero-based captured/list index; it is not itself an asset address or NFT ID. | `asset: address` — Actual backing currency address; zero denotes native ETH.<br>`amount: uint256` — Captured exact raw backing amount for the indexed leg. |

Direct payable funding is rejected.<br>Exact asset/value and before/after accounting checks; no invented backing. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `index: uint256` — Zero-based captured/list index; it is not itself an asset address or NFT ID.<br>`input1: address` — Unused source/destination-address argument retained by the common canonical ABI; authority comes from the fixed Handoff caller. | None in ABI. |

| `canonicalCapture()`<br>nonpayable | Capture the immutable cutoff for fee reserves, dust, allocation counters and optional receiver protection.<br>Requires the canonical freeze lifecycle; no ordinary admin snapshot/write. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalImport(uint256,bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Import the next source-authenticated fee reserves, dust, allocation counters and optional receiver protection record into an inert successor.<br>Enforces ordering, bounds and compatible schema; no arbitrary ledger rewrite. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `row: uint256` — Zero-based versioned canonical record row, not necessarily an NFT ordinal.<br>`record: bytes` — Exact versioned ABI-encoded canonical record authenticated against its source. | None in ABI. |

| `canonicalRestore(uint256,address)`<br>payable | Restore an actual returned backing leg for fee reserves, dust, allocation counters and optional receiver protection after abort.<br>**Native value:** Exactly zero, including native legs: the authenticated handoff transfers actual backing first, then calls this zero-value acknowledgment. Direct payable funding is rejected.<br>Restoration is not new revenue or authority to reduce original liabilities. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `index: uint256` — Zero-based captured/list index; it is not itself an asset address or NFT ID.<br>`input1: address` — Unused source/destination-address argument retained by the common canonical ABI; authority comes from the fixed Handoff caller. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalSettle()`<br>nonpayable | Complete ordered cutoff settlement for fee reserves, dust, allocation counters and optional receiver protection.<br>Some domains need no additional writes, but retain the same authenticated phase boundary. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZLPBalancer

Separately deployed fixed-controller bounded native ETH/ZZZ quote planner: full mode maximizes sampled useful main value; hybrid minimizes sampled qualifying input. No custody or generic execution.

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |

| `EXECUTION_QUALITY_VERSION()`<br>view | Read fee-adjusted execution-quality capability1; historical contracts do not acquire it from a configuration edit. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — 1 for this fresh implementation. |
| `LP_ROUTING_VERSION()`<br>view | Read full/partial LP routing interface, separate from Entry routing. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — 2; historical routing1 remains separate. |
| `MAX_ADDITIONAL_LOSS_BPS()`<br>view | Read the immutable maximum additional oracle-relative loss after known directional trading fees. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — 1000basis points (10%); independent gross and operation caps still apply. |
| `MAX_EXECUTION_POOLS()`<br>view | Read immutable bounded candidate count. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Four reviewed execution pools maximum. |

Nonempty admission revalidates prices/fees/bindings. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. Near-full no-swap deployment avoids dust-chasing. | `planReplacement(bytes32,int24,int24,uint256,uint256,uint16,uint256[4])`<br>nonpayable | Plan a bounded ordinary single or hybrid replacement whose main is physically active at the quoted stored pool tick.<br>Ordinary replacement only, with no permissionless/keeper entry to this helper. Candidates leaving the main inactive are excluded before ranking; final mint/retention guards remain. Initial/growth use plan; paused emergency stays separate. Also used for authenticated guarded same-main consolidation with current bounds, mainFloor10000 and zero replacement marks (ordinary prices are the valuation basis). No LP replacement or new keeper authority is implied by using this solver. | Sealed controller-bound Manager only; nonpayable integration surface. | `target: bytes32` — Exact admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ v4 PoolKey hash, not a pool contract address.<br>`lower: int24` — New main lower tick.<br>`upper: int24` — New main upper tick.<br>`amount0: uint256` — Available attributed ETH wei,uint128 bounded.<br>`amount1: uint256` — Available attributed ZZZ raw units,uint128 bounded.<br>`mainFloor: uint16` — 10000 selects single main;6000..9999 selects the captured hybrid main floor. Needed partial trades retain rounding headroom; an already sufficient active main needs no swap.<br>`marks: uint256[4]` — Captured Budget[price0,price1,scale0,scale1]; USD18 prices/raw decimal scales for allocation only. | `output0: (bytes32,bool,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint160,uint160,uint64,bytes32)` (struct ZZZLPBalancer.Plan) — Plan(executionPool zero for no swap,direction,raw input/quoted output/minimum,route/target post-swap Q64.96 prices,execution revision,policy fingerprint). Not a transaction or guaranteed future fill.<br>`output0.poolId: bytes32`<br>`output0.zeroForOne: bool`<br>`output0.amountIn: uint256`<br>`output0.amountOut: uint256`<br>`output0.minimumOut: uint256`<br>`output0.sqrtAfter: uint160`<br>`output0.targetSqrtAfter: uint160`<br>`output0.revision: uint64`<br>`output0.fingerprint: bytes32` |
| `quoteCandidate((address,address,uint24,int24,address),bool,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Run an authenticated full-input v4 swap quote and decode its exact custom revert; every core price/tick/fee/transient effect is rolled back.<br>QuoteResult carries output,sqrtPriceX96,stored tick with exact100-byte custom-error shape and tick-domain checks. All core price/tick/fee/transient effects revert; no funds settle or change recipient. | Only helper self-call from bounded search; other callers revert. | `key: (address,address,uint24,int24,address)` (struct PoolKey) — Typed reviewed static hookless native ETH/fixed-ZZZ PoolKey.<br>`key.currency0: address`<br>`key.currency1: address`<br>`key.fee: uint24`<br>`key.tickSpacing: int24`<br>`key.hooks: address`<br>`direction: bool` — True currency0→1, false currency1→0.<br>`amount: uint256` — Positive exact input raw units, bounded to signed-int128 maximum. | `output: uint256` — Actual quoted raw output after directional LP/protocol fees and impact.<br>`afterPrice: uint160` — Quoted post-swap sqrtPriceX96.<br>`afterTick: int24` — Actual quoted stored post-swap int24 tick, not recomputed from sqrt price; exact initialized downward crossings may be one tick lower. |
| `routePlan((bytes32,uint160,uint160,uint160,uint256,uint256,(uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint160,uint16),uint16,uint256[4]),bytes32,bool,bool)`<br>nonpayable | Evaluate one reviewed same-pair venue using bounded actual-core quotes and full/partial objective.<br>Full objective ranks useful main value; hybrid minimizes sampled qualifying input. Stored-tick activity is required only for ordinary replacement. A target-pool overshoot shrinks the bracket; a trade not yet entering the range increases it. Another venue cannot repair an already inactive target. Bounded search remains non-global; no fallback or financial limit is relaxed. | Balancer self-call only; external callers revert. | `x: (bytes32,uint160,uint160,uint160,uint256,uint256,(uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint160,uint16),uint16,uint256[4])` (struct ZZZLPBalanceMath.Context) — Context(target PoolId,Q64.96 target spot and sqrt bounds,exact ETH/ZZZ budgets,independent price bounds/scales/reference/additional-loss policy,mainFloor bps,marks[price0,price1,scale0,scale1]). Partial allocation uses captured marks only; fresh trading-price guards remain separate.<br>`x.target: bytes32`<br>`x.spot: uint160`<br>`x.lower: uint160`<br>`x.upper: uint160`<br>`x.amount0: uint256`<br>`x.amount1: uint256`<br>`x.prices: (uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint160,uint16)` (struct ZZZLPBalanceMath.Prices)<br>`x.prices.lower0: uint256`<br>`x.prices.upper0: uint256`<br>`x.prices.lower1: uint256`<br>`x.prices.upper1: uint256`<br>`x.prices.scale1: uint256`<br>`x.prices.referenceX96: uint160`<br>`x.prices.extraLoss: uint16`<br>`x.mainFloor: uint16`<br>`x.marks: uint256[4]`<br>`id: bytes32` — One admitted execution PoolId.<br>`direction: bool` — True ETH to ZZZ, false ZZZ to ETH.<br>`requireActive: bool` — True only for ordinary replacement planning: require a physically active target using actual stored ticks, including exact downward boundaries. | `best: (bytes32,bool,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint160,uint160,uint64,bytes32)` (struct ZZZLPBalancer.Plan) — Plan(executionPool zero for no swap,direction,raw input/quoted output/minimum,route/target post-swap Q64.96 prices,execution revision,policy fingerprint). Not a transaction or guaranteed future fill.<br>`best.poolId: bytes32`<br>`best.zeroForOne: bool`<br>`best.amountIn: uint256`<br>`best.amountOut: uint256`<br>`best.minimumOut: uint256`<br>`best.sqrtAfter: uint160`<br>`best.targetSqrtAfter: uint160`<br>`best.revision: uint64`<br>`best.fingerprint: bytes32`<br>`bestValue: uint256` — Useful main USD18 value at selected fixed valuation basis. |
| `unlockCallback(bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Authenticate pending quote payload/core, execute exact-input simulated swap and deliberately revert QuoteResult(output,sqrtAfter,tickAfter). | Only immutable PoolManager with the exact active pending payload. Not callable by operator/user. | `data: bytes` — Exact internally armed typed key/direction/input encoding. | `output0: bytes` — No successful output; valid execution deliberately reverts the quote payload. |

## ZZZLPPositionExecutor

Immutable Manager-created typed LP executor for a Vault-owned main and at most one same-pool residual. Atomic collection/closure; no arbitrary action, recipient, sweep or governance custody path.

[Source](../src/ZZZLPPositionExecutor.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address,address,address,address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Bind creating Manager and exact v4 dependencies. | Normally created by reviewed Manager constructor; liquidityManager is immutable constructor msg.sender, unlike controller-derived Budget/Balancer parents. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController.<br>`positionManager_: address` — Same reviewed PositionManager as parent.<br>`stateView_: address` — Same reviewed StateView.<br>`poolManager_: address` — Same reviewed PoolManager.<br>`permit2_: address` — Same Permit2 used by PositionManager.<br>`balancer_: address` — Parent's reviewed Balancer. | None in ABI. |

Exact runtime/parent bindings still require review. |

A readable foreign physical ID is not proof of Vault ownership or pool admission. | Anyone; read-only. | `id: uint256` — One physical Uniswap PositionManager NFT ID, not an aggregate, PoPL NFT or bucket ID. | `amount0: uint256` — Rounded-down raw principal currency0; wei ETH only for an admitted native pair.<br>`amount1: uint256` — Rounded-down raw principal currency1; fixed ZZZ only for an admitted native pair. |

Zero has explicit empty identity for parked contexts. | `output0: bytes32` — Hash of executor/PosM/Vault,pool key,main/residual IDs and packed bounds; mutable L excluded. |

Zero alone does not prove an arbitrary ID is valid. |

| `close(bytes32,uint256,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Burn both registered legs atomically and return exact pair proceeds only to Vault.<br>Per-leg raw minima remain. Clear residual association/count only after successful burns. Vault separately harvests fees before principal close and applies maintenance/terminal accounting. Any second-leg failure reverts both. | Only sealed current immutable Manager; nonreentrant. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ v4 PoolKey hash, not a pool contract address.<br>`id: uint256` — Current registered main Uniswap LP NFT ID; resolve the optional residual separately.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix seconds: block.timestamp &lt;= deadline &lt;= block.timestamp+600 at execution. | None in ABI. |
| `closeSecondary(bytes32,uint256,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Burn only the main's registered residual, returning exact ETH/ZZZ proceeds to Vault.<br>The main is not burned or increased. Actual registered residual may be mixed or one-sided; fixed typed BURN/TAKE actions and bounded raw per-leg minima remain. Association/count clear only after successful burn. Vault must harvest fees first; governance does not call this lower-level function directly. No arbitrary LP ID, recipient, swap or sweep is admitted. | Only its immutable current sealed Manager while globally paused; nonreentrant, pause rechecked after callbacks. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ v4 PoolKey hash; not a pool contract address.<br>`main: uint256` — Actual registered main Uniswap LP NFT ID. The operation resolves its sole associated residual.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix seconds: block.timestamp &lt;= deadline &lt;= block.timestamp+600 at execution. | None in ABI. |
| `collect(bytes32,uint256,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Collect main/residual fees atomically without removing principal; exact receipts go only to Vault.<br>Vault allocates80/10/5/5 after actual bucket receipt; collection does not itself compound,buy back or claim. | Only sealed current immutable Manager; nonreentrant. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ v4 PoolKey hash, not a pool contract address.<br>`id: uint256` — Current registered main Uniswap LP NFT ID; resolve the optional residual separately.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix seconds: block.timestamp &lt;= deadline &lt;= block.timestamp+600 at execution. | None in ABI. |
| `increase(bytes32,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint128,uint256)`<br>payable | Grow authenticated main at unchanged bounds; residual untouched.<br>**Native value:** Exactly amount0 ETH forwarded by Manager.<br>Check exact main L delta and preserve unspent/prefunded balances and residual. | Only sealed current immutable Manager; nonreentrant. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ v4 PoolKey hash, not a pool contract address.<br>`id: uint256` — Current registered main Uniswap LP NFT ID; resolve the optional residual separately.<br>`amount0: uint256` — Exact available ETH wei.<br>`amount1: uint256` — Exact available ZZZ raw units, approved to this child.<br>`minimumLiquidity: uint128` — Minimum added MAIN liquidity units.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix seconds: block.timestamp &lt;= deadline &lt;= block.timestamp+600 at execution. | `spent0: uint256` — Actual ETH wei spent.<br>`spent1: uint256` — Actual ZZZ raw units spent. |
| `mint(bytes32,int24,int24,uint256,uint256,uint128,uint256)`<br>payable | Mint Vault-owned main and optional residual from only the supplied attributed budget.<br>**Native value:** Exactly amount0 ETH forwarded by Manager.<br>Residual only during enabled normal replacement, never first activation/compound/emergency. Ordinary hybrid main must actually be in-range at mint. Verify exact debts/final spot/key/L and return unused funds only to Vault. Both NFTs remain Vault-owned; small rounding leftovers stay attributed idle. Hybrid main physical mark and lower&lt;=tick&lt;upper are rechecked after all external execution/refund callbacks; a callback that leaves it inactive atomically reverts. Normal whole-position replacement requires an actually active main range before and after all mint/refund callbacks in both single and hybrid modes. This does not prohibit already-authorized initial/growth out-of-range contributions or reinterpret the explicit paused emergency manifest. | Only immutable creating Manager while sealed current LIQUIDITY module; nonreentrant. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted native ETH/fixed-ZZZ v4 PoolKey hash, not a pool contract address.<br>`lower: int24` — Reviewed main lower tick.<br>`upper: int24` — Reviewed main upper tick.<br>`amount0: uint256` — Exact available ETH wei.<br>`amount1: uint256` — Exact available ZZZ raw units, approved by Vault to this child.<br>`minimumLiquidity: uint128` — Minimum MAIN liquidity units, not aggregate L/USD.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix seconds: block.timestamp &lt;= deadline &lt;= block.timestamp+600 at execution. | `tokenId: uint256` — New main LP ID; residual from secondaryId(main).<br>`spent0: uint256` — Actual ETH wei spent across both legs.<br>`spent1: uint256` — Actual ZZZ raw units spent across both legs. |

Forced/prefunded balances are separate from measured operation credit. | Only currently armed nativeSender; otherwise revert. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZLPRewardConverter

Fixed LP rewards-ledger-only executor converting earned ERC20 balances into native ETH. Shares the abstract ZZZRewardConverter implementation, but has its own address, immutable ledger, route policies and storage; no shared custody or new authority.

[Source](../src/ZZZLPRewardConverter.sol). Category: Project contract.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

## ZZZLiquidityManager

Immutable native ETH/ZZZ swap coordinator with reviewed Balancer and fixed typed LPPositionExecutor. Vault owns both LP NFTs; exact LP/ZZZ deployment approvals target the child, while swaps use Manager budget approvals.

[Source](../src/ZZZLiquidityManager.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| `balance(bytes32,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256)`<br>payable | Atomically balance only the supplied budget using zero or one swap selected from reviewed venues; return exact remaining ETH/ZZZ amounts for subsequent LP deployment.<br>**Native value:** Exactly amount0 native wei. Network gas is separate.<br>Only explicit supplied entrant/maintenance budget is used. Existing-main contribution/compound plans preserve residual and clock; normal enabled replacement uses captured Budget marks for minimum-main sizing. No-swap already at60% needs no route. Actual output,refund,prefund,context/physical state and post-swap price checks remain; final actual NAV/utilization/loss guards are separate. Guarded paused same-main consolidation uses the existing active-aware route solver for current main bounds, mainFloor10000 and ordinary price marks. Only the fixed Migration active context selects it. Ordinary full replacement keeps its existing active solver; other growth/compound existing-ID paths retain ordinary planning; explicit emergency routing/mask remains unchanged. | Only fixed authorized Vault; sealed/current module, short deadline and enabled native target. No arbitrary recipient. | `targetPool: bytes32` — Actual native ETH/fixed-ZZZ LP destination pool ID.<br>`existingLpId: uint256` — Existing Vault-owned physical LP NFT ID, or zero to plan a new target range. | `endEmergency()`<br>nonpayable | Validate and clear Manager temporary context after the same atomic Vault operation. | Actual Vault module only; valid scoped paused Budget context. Nonreentrant. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `increase(bytes32,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint128,uint256)`<br>payable | Grow only existing main through fixed child, preserving residual/ranges/rebalance clock.<br>**Native value:** Exactly amount0 ETH.<br>Child is exact LP/ZZZ spender; actual spend and main L delta verified. Both legs remain in NAV. | Only fixed Vault. | `pool: bytes32` — Exact admitted bytes32 hash of the v4 PoolKey, not a pool contract address.<br>`id: uint256` — Physical Uniswap position-manager NFT ID, not a PoPL NFT or bucket ID.<br>`a0: uint256` — Raw units of actual ordered currency0; native ETH uses wei.<br>`a1: uint256` — Raw units of actual ordered currency1.<br>`minL: uint128` — Minimum actual Uniswap liquidity units to create/add; not token units or USD.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix timestamp in seconds; must satisfy this operation’s freshness/deadline guards. | `output0: uint256` — Actual raw currency0 spent increasing the existing LP.<br>`output1: uint256` — Actual raw currency1 spent increasing the existing LP. |
| `mint(bytes32,int24,int24,uint256,uint256,uint128,uint256)`<br>payable | Mint a Vault-owned main and optional residual through immutable typed child.<br>**Native value:** Exactly amount0 ETH for admitted native pair, forwarded to fixed child.<br>Vault approves exact ZZZ and each resulting LP NFT only to positionOperator, never approve-all. Minimum L is main-only; spent0/1 cover both legs. Residual requires normal captured hybrid context and an actually in-range new main. First activation/compound/emergency cannot acquire that permission. Actual debts/spot/context, ownership and final Budget utilization/loss guards apply. Hybrid main physical mark and lower&lt;=tick&lt;upper are rechecked after all external execution/refund callbacks; a callback that leaves it inactive atomically reverts. Normal whole-position replacement requires an actually active main range before and after all mint/refund callbacks in both single and hybrid modes. This does not prohibit already-authorized initial/growth out-of-range contributions or reinterpret the explicit paused emergency manifest. | Only the fixed Vault. | `pool: bytes32` — Exact admitted bytes32 hash of the v4 PoolKey, not a pool contract address.<br>`lower: int24` — Lower Uniswap tick, not a human-readable percentage.<br>`upper: int24` — Upper Uniswap tick, aligned to admitted spacing.<br>`a0: uint256` — Raw units of actual ordered currency0; native ETH uses wei.<br>`a1: uint256` — Raw units of actual ordered currency1.<br>`minL: uint128` — Minimum actual Uniswap liquidity units to create/add; not token units or USD.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix timestamp in seconds; must satisfy this operation’s freshness/deadline guards. | `id: uint256` — New MAIN LP ID; residual from secondaryId(main).<br>`spent0: uint256` — Actual ETH wei spent across both legs.<br>`spent1: uint256` — Actual ZZZ raw units spent across both legs. |
| `swap(bytes32,bool,uint256,uint256,uint256)`<br>payable | Execute a typed exact-input swap in an admitted pool with price/output/deadline checks.<br>**Native value:** Exactly amountIn when the selected swap input is native ETH; otherwise zero.<br>No arbitrary recipient: outputs go to the authenticated caller; exact native call value and approval cleanup apply. Inputs: zeroForOne selects ordered currency0→1; raw input/minimumOut and Unix deadline bound execution. Returns: Actual raw output amount. A zero minimum rejects. A positive caller minimum below protocol policy is raised to the stronger protocol floor rather than rejected solely for underspecification; stronger caller minima still bind. Full policy context is captured before input pull and rechecked after output. Direct swaps now also require a constructible configured target range; extreme TickMath ratios can prevent them. | Only the sealed fixed Vault or Buyback module. | `poolId: bytes32` — Exact admitted bytes32 hash of the v4 PoolKey, not a pool contract address.<br>`zeroForOne: bool` — True swaps currency0→currency1; false swaps currency1→currency0.<br>`amountIn: uint256` — Exact raw units of the chosen swap input currency.<br>`minimumOut: uint256` — Minimum raw units of the ordered swap output currency.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix timestamp in seconds; must satisfy this operation’s freshness/deadline guards. | `amountOut: uint256` — Actual raw output amount. |
| `unlockCallback(bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Settle the exact outstanding swap inside the pinned v4 PoolManager unlock callback.<br>Not an arbitrary public swap or executable-call surface. Returns: ABI-encoded measured swap output. | Only bound PoolManager with the matching one-time pending payload hash. | `data: bytes` — Exact pending ABI payload; callback authentication binds its hash. | `output0: bytes` — ABI-encoded measured swap output. |

## ZZZMigrationBudget

Standalone fixed-controller Vault companion for captured contribution/maintenance accounting. Aggregate both legs, freeze original-source/target controls, prove replacement utilization and retain exact reserve/custody separation.

[Source](../src/ZZZMigrationBudget.sol). Category: Project contract.

## ZZZNFT

Canonical ERC721 ownership, asynchronous fixed-artwork draws, activation royalty rights and pre-seal-bound SeaDrop callbacks. Read-only rendering is separately deployed and immutable; primary proceeds never become LP or royalties.

[Source](../src/ZZZNFT.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,string,address)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy the canonical NFT with fixed royalty ledger, claim router and predeployed metadata renderer. | Deployment only; reviewed matching controller/factory and renderer required. | `controller: address` — Exact collection controller.<br>`baseURI: string` — Reviewed immutable IPFS/Arweave artwork root; empty draft may be populated once before seal.<br>`renderer: address` — Predeployed reviewed ZZZNFTMetadata with code and the SAME controller; no later renderer setter. | None in ABI. |

### Write functions

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `addRoyaltyAsset(address)`<br>nonpayable | Append an admitted ERC20 royalty payment currency to the NFT-bound ledger. Reviewed non-rebasing/exact-transfer currency required; prefunds are quarantined and append-only history/old cohort entitlements persist. This does not admit an activation token or force marketplace payment. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. | `token: address` — token is the actual ERC20 royalty currency; native ETH is already admitted. | None in ABI. |
| `approve(address,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Set or clear the one-token ERC721 transfer approval. Approval permits NFT transfer, not contribution/claim authority or principal redemption. The NFT’s unclaimed rights follow any later transfer. | NFT owner or owner-approved transfer operator. | `to: address` — Address granted one-token transfer approval; zero clears that token’s approval.<br>`tokenId: uint256` — Existing canonical NFT ID whose transfer approval is changed. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalRetire()`<br>nonpayable | Emit the terminal metadata update after coordinator retirement. Notification does not independently retire or reset the collection; terminal ownership/custody rules remain. | Bound canonical handoff only, canonical state 3. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalWriteArtwork(uint8,uint256,uint16)`<br>nonpayable | Import one sparse draw-map slot into the inert replacement. Internal authenticated migration state only; not an administrative artwork editor. | Fixed NFT canonical companion only, sealed importing state 2. | `rarity: uint8` — Source draw rarity index 0/1/2.<br>`item: uint256` — Exact sparse inventory slot authenticated by the canonical companion.<br>`value: uint16` — Source encoded uint16 sparse-draw value: zero denotes identity; nonzero encodes the replacement entry plus one. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalWriteHeader(uint256,uint256,uint256,uint16[3],uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Import the source NFT draw header into an inert replacement. Internal migration endpoint; no operator reset/requota function. The companion validates the exact source export.<br>V10 original-buyer/stage books restore from subsequent ordered records; aggregate header count alone does not complete handoff. | Fixed NFT canonical companion only, sealed importing state 2. | `count: uint256` — Source mintedCount, preserving the exact issued supply.<br>`next: uint256` — Source nextToFinalize, a one-based mint ordinal, not NFT ID.<br>`remaining: uint256` — Source remainingToReveal, including finite entries that are still unminted.<br>`quotas: uint16[3]` — Exact remaining [Commun,Rare,Legendary] quota counts as uint16[3].<br>`epoch: uint256` — Source randomnessEpoch used to preserve original request-identity namespaces. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalWriteRecord(uint256,(uint256,address,uint8,uint8,uint8,uint8,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,address,uint8))`<br>nonpayable | Import an exact V10 owner, trait, original draw, original buyer and issuance-stage record.<br>Inert ownership import without receiver callbacks or new requests. Helper books restore in the same ordered write; rejected records roll back both. Old V9 ten-field records are not reinterpreted. | Fixed NFT canonical companion only, sealed importing state2. | `row: uint256` — One-based authenticated SOURCE mint ordinal.<br>`value: (uint256,address,uint8,uint8,uint8,uint8,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,address,uint8)` (struct CanonicalNFTRecord) — CanonicalNFTRecord in ABI order: id,currentOwner,rarity,color,palette,pattern,requestId,requestEpoch,rarityWord,colorWord,ORIGINAL mint recipient,mintStage. Preserve current owner separately from original buyer; stage1/2/3 reconstructs helper quotas.<br>`value.id: uint256`<br>`value.owner: address`<br>`value.rarity: uint8`<br>`value.color: uint8`<br>`value.palette: uint8`<br>`value.pattern: uint8`<br>`value.requestId: uint256`<br>`value.requestEpoch: uint256`<br>`value.rarityWord: uint256`<br>`value.colorWord: uint256`<br>`value.originalRecipient: address`<br>`value.mintStage: uint8` | None in ABI. |
| `finalizeTraits(uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Finalize the next mint-ordered revealed rarity/palette/pattern from its verified stored words. Irreversible draw without replacement. Requires existing NFT, fulfilled original request, traits not already ready and exactly nextToFinalize ordinal; preserves exact 600/300/100 quotas. | Anyone; canonical accounting must be operating. | `id: uint256` — id is the actual NFT ID, not its ordinal. | None in ABI. |
| `increaseRoyaltyAssetCapacity(uint8,bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Increase reviewed royalty currency capacity.<br>No currency removal, transfer, swap or reweighting. After sealing: current governance; pre/post canConfigure checks. | `capacity: uint8` — New ERC20 capacity greater than current, at most64.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero exact review fingerprint after gas/API/history/canonical capacity rehearsal. | None in ABI. |
| `mint(address,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Issue a dormant NFT and original asynchronous draw only in the explicitly unbound direct-mint test configuration; a bound SeaDrop helper rejects this legacy path. | Bound Vault module only and no SeaDrop helper; not the production/OpenSea primary mint path. | `to: address` — Recipient chosen by the authorized Vault issuance path.<br>`id: uint256` — New canonical hashed NFT identity assigned by Vault; not a user-selected sequential token number. | None in ABI. |
| `mintSeaDrop(address,uint256)`<br>payable | Receive an authenticated SeaDrop mint callback and create dormant canonical NFTs plus original VRF requests.<br>**Native value:** Exactly0 wei. The buyer pays gross native ETH to SeaDrop, which settles creator/platform amounts after this callback. Never call this directly as a wallet.<br>Reserves all books, records each hashed identity through the NFT-only zero-value Vault branch, issues each original draw, then revalidates. Failures revert the whole upstream mint/payment. Later authorized SeaDrop payee callbacks affect future settings only; no invented post-settlement callback exists. | Fixed reviewed SeaDrop through the bound helper only; sealed/live/operating/nonterminal and nonReentrant. | `minter: address` — Original beneficiary validated by upstream proof/payment; current owner can later differ.<br>`quantity: uint256` — Complete batch quantity within configured engineering, phase, wallet and global limits. | None in ABI. |
| `notifyContribution(uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Register persistent activated royalty eligibility after successful Vault share issuance and notify metadata. Requires revealed NFT and positive shares. Rarity determines fixed weight 1/2/3; this does not collect a new royalty payment or transfer LP principal. | Bound Vault only; internal contribution completion endpoint. | `id: uint256` — id is the successfully funded NFT ID. | None in ABI. |
| `policyMetadataChanged()`<br>nonpayable | Emit collection-wide ERC4906 metadata invalidation after cosmetic threshold changes.<br>BatchMetadataUpdate(0,uint256 max) requests external refresh; no marketplace cache timing guarantee. | Only fixed Access.protocolPolicy address; event only,no economic state or ownership mutation. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `rawFulfillRandomWords(uint256,uint256[])`<br>nonpayable | Store an authenticated original two-word provider result without drawing traits inside the callback. Unknown/duplicate/wrong-length results revert. | None in ABI. |
| `refreshMetadata(uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Emit an ERC4906 notification for an existing NFT. No valuation read, artwork generation, funds movement or guarantee of immediate marketplace cache refresh. | Anyone; no NFT ownership required. | `id: uint256` — id is NFT ID. | None in ABI. |
| `refreshMetadataRange(uint256,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Emit one ERC4906 notification per actual NFT in a bounded ordinal interval. Ordinals are not token IDs. | Anyone. | `firstOrdinal: uint256` — First one-based minted ordinal, inclusive.<br>`lastOrdinal: uint256` — Last inclusive minted ordinal&lt;=mintedCount; last-first&lt;50. These are ordinals, not NFT IDs. | None in ABI. |
| `safeTransferFrom(address,address,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Transfer the NFT and all its associated LP allocation and unclaimed economic rights to the next holder. No individual principal redemption during ordinary operation. After collective exit, unpaid principal and remaining rewards follow current ownership; already paid principal does not. Safe overloads validate ERC721Receiver on contract recipients; transferFrom does not, so incompatible contract custody may trap the NFT. Frozen/importing/retired/cancelled ordinary transfers revert. | Owner, token-approved address or approved-for-all operator; canonical accounting must be operating. | `from: address` — Current canonical NFT owner.<br>`to: address` — Nonzero next owner; safe overloads require compatible ERC721Receiver for contract recipients.<br>`tokenId: uint256` — Existing canonical NFT ID, carrying attached LP allocation and all accrued unclaimed rights. | None in ABI. |
| `safeTransferFrom(address,address,uint256,bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Transfer the NFT and all its associated LP allocation and unclaimed economic rights to the next holder. No individual principal redemption during ordinary operation. After collective exit, unpaid principal and remaining rewards follow current ownership; already paid principal does not. Safe overloads validate ERC721Receiver on contract recipients; transferFrom does not, so incompatible contract custody may trap the NFT. ## ZZZNFTCanonical

Fixed V10 ownership/artwork/draw codec plus original-buyer and issuance-stage quota restoration; no custody or independent economic authority.

[Source](../src/ZZZNFTCanonical.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy and bind Seal-bound ownership/trait/inventory export/import codec; no fungible custody domain. | Deployment only; required creator/controller/parent checks apply before this instance can be bound/sealed. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController address for this deployment’s roles and lifecycle.<br>`collection: address` — Existing NFT contract bound to the same controller; canonical codec does not create a second collection. | None in ABI. |

### Read functions

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `access()`<br>view | Identify the fixed controller that supplies this component’s module, governance and lifecycle authorization.<br>Reading the controller address grants no authority. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract IZZZAccess) — The fixed controller that supplies this component’s module, governance and lifecycle authorization. |
| `canonicalAssetCount()`<br>pure | Report that the NFT ownership codec has no fungible backing legs.<br>Returns: Always zero; actual principal/rewards belong to other domains. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — Always zero; actual principal/rewards belong to other domains. |
| `canonicalCount()`<br>view | Read the version-specific exported record count for NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory.<br>Record rows are not necessarily NFTs; headers/assets/indices may add rows. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — The version-specific exported record count for NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory. |
| `canonicalExport(uint256)`<br>view | Encode one exact captured NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory record.<br>V10 NFT rows append original recipient and phase while retaining the actual CURRENT owner; remaining artwork rows and finalized draw semantics remain. Historical V9 records are incompatible. | Anyone; read-only. | `row: uint256` — Zero-based versioned canonical record row, not necessarily an NFT ordinal. | `output0: bytes` — Encode one exact captured NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory record. |
| `canonicalValidate()`<br>view | Assert complete imported/restored record and backing consistency for NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory.<br>Reverts on inconsistency; a successful call alone does not activate a successor or approve release. | Anyone; read-only assertion. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `deploymentConfigurationCommitment()`<br>view | Read V10 local policy and fixed mint-helper deployment commitments. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: bytes32` — Hash binding ProtocolPolicy identity/runtime/configuration and optional helper identity/runtime/local settings plus singleton identity/runtime; not a transaction approval. |
| `nft()`<br>view | Identify the immutable NFT whose canonical ownership/trait records this codec serves. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` (contract IZZZNFTCanonicalState) — The immutable NFT whose canonical ownership/trait records this codec serves. |

### Write functions

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `canonicalActivate()`<br>nonpayable | Activate the validated imported NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory state.<br>Requires its authenticated funded cutover; cannot be used as a generic unpause. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalCapture()`<br>nonpayable | Capture the immutable cutoff for NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory.<br>Requires the canonical freeze lifecycle; no ordinary admin snapshot/write. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalImport(uint256,bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Import the next source-authenticated NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory record into an inert successor.<br>Requires ordered V10 bytes and matching helper/singleton/semantic mint settings. Restores original-buyer lifetime and phase books, never reconstructs them from current ownership. Incomplete imports cannot activate. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `row: uint256` — Zero-based versioned canonical record row, not necessarily an NFT ordinal.<br>`record: bytes` — Exact versioned ABI-encoded canonical record authenticated against its source. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalSettle()`<br>nonpayable | Complete ordered cutoff settlement for NFT ownership, immutable traits and remaining artwork inventory.<br>Some domains need no additional writes, but retain the same authenticated phase boundary. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |

Existing safeguard cannot later be removed via setSequencerFeed. | None in ABI. |

Every funded bucket remains returnable to holders as actual ETH + ZZZ.<br>Only native ETH/fixed ZZZ is admitted. During collective Closing/Allocating this remains locked. After Migration.exitPhase()==Ready(3), governance may validate/re-enable or admit a swap venue only with depositsEnabled=false. Ready means principal allocated; holder payouts may remain. All existing native/runtime/feed/reward-asset/parameter checks remain. Venue admission neither spends reserves nor reopens mint, activation, growth or principal; lpExitStarted remains irreversible. Width must exceed retained trigger&gt;=100bps and max deviation; near-extreme tick-rounded ranges may be unrepresentable. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. Protocol fee is separate.; key.tickSpacing: Positive signed-int24 v4 tick spacing; must match the actual initialized pool.; key.hooks: Must be address zero for the currently supported hookless venues.; depositsEnabled: Desired contribution acceptance; only one pool may be true at a time. Must be false for post-exit Ready-phase reserve venues.; operationsEnabled: Desired ordinary maintenance/deployment acceptance; does not erase custody/history.; rangeBps: Price width in basis points; 100..9999, with orientation selected separately by Strategy.; triggerBps: Retained historical policy1 movement field, at least100 and below rangeBps; policy2 never uses it to authorize ordinary replacement.; maxSpotDeviationBps: Allowed spot-versus-oracle deviation in BPS, 1..500 and below rangeBps.; maxSlippageBps: Per-venue swap minimum guard in BPS, 1..1000 and sufficient for static LP fee. Separate from aggregate contribution/maintenance caps.; minRebalanceInterval: Cooldown seconds, 600..604800.; minDepositUSD: Pool minimum in USD18 (1 USD=10**18); actual input must satisfy both current ProtocolPolicy action minimum and this stronger pool minimum.; maxPositionUSD: Maximum cumulative admitted gross contribution per NFT in USD18, at least minDepositUSD; not current NAV or a total pool cap.<br>`config.key: (address,address,uint24,int24,address)` (struct PoolKey)<br>`config.key.currency0: address`<br>`config.key.currency1: address`<br>`config.key.fee: uint24`<br>`config.key.tickSpacing: int24`<br>`config.key.hooks: address`<br>`config.depositsEnabled: bool`<br>`config.operationsEnabled: bool`<br>`config.rangeBps: uint16`<br>`config.triggerBps: uint16`<br>`config.maxSpotDeviationBps: uint16`<br>`config.maxSlippageBps: uint16`<br>`config.minRebalanceInterval: uint32`<br>`config.minDepositUSD: uint256`<br>`config.maxPositionUSD: uint256` | `poolId: bytes32` — Actual PoolKey-derived poolId; eligible future fee tiers remain possible. |

True means DISABLE, false leaves the existing state unchanged. At least one flag must be true. Disabling deposits clears this pool as contributionPool. Re-enable only through full configureNative validation; post-exit re-admission requires Ready and deposits disabled. | Before sealing: only the current bootstrap address, not a distinct governance wallet. After sealing: only current governance with canonicalState=0 and no preparation. No waiting period; ordinary pause alone does not block configuration. | `poolId: bytes32` — Admitted exact PoolKey hash.<br>`disableDeposits: bool` — True only disables; false retains current flag. True also clears this current contribution pool.<br>`disableOperations: bool` — True only disables ordinary operations; false retains current flag. At least one disable flag must be true. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZPortfolioPolicy

Fixed noncustodial desired single/hybrid mode,85%-edge trigger,per-pool main allocation/residual width/gap and scoped revisions. No setter moves backing; actual guarded placement applies changes.

[Source](../src/ZZZPortfolioPolicy.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

## ZZZPythPriceFeed

Optional immutable Pyth-to-AggregatorV3 reader; not a publisher, paid update service, feed admission or independence proof.

[Source](../src/ZZZPythPriceFeed.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,bytes32,bytes32,int32,uint32,uint16,bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy one immutable reader for an exact Pyth product and observation policy. | Anyone may deploy; deployment does not admit it to Oracle or qualify upstream proxy/independence. | `source: address` — Reviewed Pyth Core contract with code.<br>`codeHash: bytes32` — Exact nonzero Core runtime hash; does not identify a proxy implementation.<br>`id: bytes32` — Exact nonzero reviewed USD feed/product ID.<br>`exponent: int32` — Fixed expected Pyth exponent from -18 through 0; reader decimals=-exponent.<br>`age: uint32` — Maximum original publish age in seconds, 1..86400.<br>`confidenceBps: uint16` — Maximum confidence/positive-price ratio, 0..300 basis points.<br>`review: bytes32` — Nonzero review commitment; not a proof of an independent audit. | None in ABI. |

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `SOURCE_CALL_GAS()`<br>view | Read the 300,000-gas maximum for a bounded upstream Pyth read; not a flat service fee. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint256` — 300000 gas ceiling for the exact128-byte getPriceUnsafe response; no update transaction is submitted. |
| `core()`<br>view | Read the immutable reviewed Pyth Core address. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: address` — Immutable reviewed Pyth Core source address; upstream proxy controls remain an external qualification. |
| `decimals()`<br>view | Read the AggregatorV3 answer precision derived from the fixed negative Pyth exponent. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint8` — AggregatorV3-shaped precision equal to minus expectedExponent,0..18; observation values retain that scale. |
| `expectedCoreCodeHash()`<br>view | Read the exact immutable upstream runtime pin; proxy implementation safety remains a separate review. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: bytes32` — Immutable expected outer source runtime hash checked on each observation. |
| `expectedExponent()`<br>view | Read the immutable expected exponent in [-18,0]; it must match each observation. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: int32` — Immutable accepted Pyth decimal exponent in[-18,0]; changed exponent rejects rather than rescaling silently. |
| `feedId()`<br>view | Read the immutable Pyth USD product/feed identity. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: bytes32` — Immutable nonzero Pyth feed identifier, not a token symbol or proof of market independence. |
| `latestRoundData()`<br>view | Read a bounded original Pyth observation in AggregatorV3 format. Read-only: does not pay for or publish Pyth updates. | `maxAge()`<br>view | Read the immutable maximum original publish age, in seconds. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint32` — Immutable allowed observation age in seconds, positive and at most86400; publishTime supplies age. |
| `maxConfidenceBps()`<br>view | Read the immutable allowed confidence/positive-price ratio in bps. | Anyone; read-only. | None in ABI. | `output0: uint16` — Immutable allowed confidence/positive-price ratio in basis points,0..300; zero requires zero confidence width. |

## ZZZRebalanceConfirmation

Standalone fixed-controller noncustodial receipt-backed sampled threshold confirmation. Observe during cooldown,check readiness separately. No funds/approvals/execution and no continuous-history or MEV guarantee.

Read/lifecycle faults fail closed. Policy/geometry/physical-identity changes invalidate samples. Compare the reviewed payload and runtime bindings before NFT deployment; corruption makes create revert. |

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `create()`<br>nonpayable | Deploy a fresh caller-bound royalty receiver and bind its canonical controller atomically.<br>Checks exact part sizes/hashes, copies only data after each three-byte REVERT prefix, checks the original whole payload hash, appends only the typed caller NFT and binds the fixed controller atomically. Ordinary mint/claim never reads the children. Data storage reduces deployment gas; exact target gas still requires qualification. | Permissionless caller; normal NFT construction uses this path. | None in ABI. | `receiver: address` (contract ZZZRoyaltyRewards) — New receiver contract address, permanently bound to msg.sender as its NFT. |

Direct payable funding is rejected.<br>Exact asset/value and before/after accounting checks; no invented backing. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `i: uint256` — Zero-based captured backing leg index.<br>`input1: address` — Unused source/destination-address argument retained by the common canonical ABI; authority comes from the fixed Handoff caller. | None in ABI. |
| `canonicalActivate()`<br>nonpayable | Activate the validated imported rarity-weighted cohorts, exact whole rights and reviewed unallocated balances state.<br>Requires its authenticated funded cutover; cannot be used as a generic unpause. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |

LP emergency/terminal paths stay independent. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | None in ABI. | None in ABI. |

No source semantics inferred from tuple size. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `row: uint256` — Zero-based versioned canonical record row, not necessarily an NFT ordinal.<br>`record: bytes` — Exact versioned ABI-encoded canonical record authenticated against its source. | None in ABI. |

This hook cannot be called by governance directly; reviewed target admission/cohort and pre/post controls are enforced by the coordinator. | Fixed canonical coordinator only; cancelled state4; nonReentrant. | `asset: address` — One original currency; zero for native ETH.<br>`receiver: address` — Verified active royalty ledger supplied only by the fixed coordinator. | `amount: uint256` — Exact raw units transferred; not an external custody payout. |

Direct payable funding is rejected.<br>Restoration is not new revenue or authority to reduce original liabilities. | Only fixed CanonicalHandoff. | `i: uint256` — Zero-based captured backing leg index.<br>`input1: address` — Unused source/destination-address argument retained by the common canonical ABI; authority comes from the fixed Handoff caller. | None in ABI. |

In raw units of the specified currency. |
| `forwardLate(address)`<br>nonpayable | Forward admitted or unsupported late currencies from a retired receiver to its authenticated immediate successor only.<br>No caller-selected recipient or active-source drain. A healthy exact-transfer unsupported currency can forward without admission; the active successor separately admits or recovers it. Unreadable/nonstandard tokens can still fail. | Permissionless after authenticated canonical retirement. | `asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths. | `amount: uint256` — Raw late amount forwarded. |

| `sync(address)`<br>nonpayable | Recognize actual newly arrived royalty funds and advance that currency’s eligible-weight index.<br>Recovered optional assets first allocate unseen old receipts to the old cohort, then enroll waiting NFTs. Returns: Raw amount recognized, not an individual payout. | Permissionless in active economic state. | `asset: address` — Actual currency address; zero denotes native ETH in native-aware paths. | `amount: uint256` — Raw amount recognized, not an individual payout. |

## ZZZSeaDropMint

Pre-seal-bound, noncustodial SeaDrop V1 policy and exact original-buyer/phase issuance books. No proceeds receiver, NFT mint, arbitrary target or executor. Separate mint-helper code and the singleton runtime require deployment review.

[Source](../src/ZZZSeaDropMint.sol). Category: Project contract.

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `beginMint(address,address,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Reserve the complete admitted batch before any VRF or ERC721 receiver callback.<br>Requires live sealed operating/nonterminal state, exact source/runtime and coherent upstream getters. Updates all batch counts and original-recipient/stage rows before callbacks. The enclosing NFT transaction provides atomic rollback. | Fixed NFT only; internal parent workflow, never an operator transaction. | `caller: address` — Actual caller forwarded by NFT; must equal its immutable SeaDrop singleton.<br>`recipient: address` — Nonzero original mint beneficiary authenticated by upstream payment/proof rules.<br>`quantity: uint256` — Positive quantity within configured maxBatch and all current phase, wallet and supply limits. | `firstOrdinal: uint256` — First one-based mint ordinal reserved by this batch.<br>`fingerprint: bytes32` — Exact in-flight fingerprint used by Vault identity recording and finishMint.<br>`phase: uint8` — Current phase1,2 or3. |
| `configure((uint16,uint16,uint16,uint16,uint8,uint48,uint48,uint48,uint48,uint48,uint48,bool,bytes32,address,bytes32),uint128,bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Capture reviewed future stage settings and an explicit current price without changing upstream SeaDrop.<br>Pause new minting while synchronizing matching NFT-keyed upstream setters and royalty mint end. Current positive proof roots cannot silently change relevant leaf terms; a reviewed new root is required. This guard does not decode arbitrary Merkle leaves or recognize all previously used roots. Free-team-only roots need not change for an unrelated paid public-price change. Historical allocations and original-buyer counts persist. | Governance only after seal; bootstrap before seal. Operating/canonical/terminal-exit guards apply. | `next: (uint16,uint16,uint16,uint16,uint8,uint48,uint48,uint48,uint48,uint48,uint48,bool,bytes32,address,bytes32)` (struct ZZZSeaDropMint.MintConfig) — MintConfig in ABI order: teamAllocation (initial20 free units), allowlistAllocation (initial500 paid units), publicWalletLimit (lifetime count), feeBps (reviewed paid platform basis points), maxBatch (1..10), teamStart/teamEnd, allowlistStart/allowlistEnd, publicStart/publicEnd (inclusive UNIX seconds with strictly disjoint stages), releaseUnusedTeamToPublic (initialfalse; retain unminted team reserve), merkleRoot (reviewed free-team/paid-allowlist proofs; zero only when both allocations are zero), feeRecipient (exact approved platform address), allowedPayersHash (Keccak of ABI-encoded sorted bounded reviewed payer array, including explicit empty array). Team+allowlist&lt;=1000; issued counts never reset. Unused allowlist capacity rolls into public.<br>`next.teamAllocation: uint16`<br>`next.allowlistAllocation: uint16`<br>`next.publicWalletLimit: uint16`<br>`next.feeBps: uint16`<br>`next.maxBatch: uint8`<br>`next.teamStart: uint48`<br>`next.teamEnd: uint48`<br>`next.allowlistStart: uint48`<br>`next.allowlistEnd: uint48`<br>`next.publicStart: uint48`<br>`next.publicEnd: uint48`<br>`next.releaseUnusedTeamToPublic: bool`<br>`next.merkleRoot: bytes32`<br>`next.feeRecipient: address`<br>`next.allowedPayersHash: bytes32`<br>`expectedMintPrice: uint128` — Exact expected current ProtocolPolicy paid mint price in wei; a price change before execution rejects this configuration.<br>`reviewHash: bytes32` — Nonzero source-bound manifest review hash; independently verify every proof/leaf and intended platform terms. | None in ABI. |
| `finishMint(bytes32)`<br>nonpayable | Revalidate and release the NFT callback batch reservation.<br>Checks unchanged controls and NFT/helper count equality. Never call manually or add a later settlement transaction; free and paid callbacks both finish before SeaDrop payout callbacks. | Fixed NFT only; internal parent workflow, never an operator transaction. | `fingerprint: bytes32` — Exact fingerprint returned by beginMint within THIS same NFT call. | None in ABI. |
| `prepareUpdate(address,bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Validate one of six fixed typed NFT setters and encode its corresponding upstream call.<br>Rejects an active batch, wrong singleton, unrecognized setter or unauthorized caller. Revision increments before the NFT executes the returned call; failed external calls roll the revision back. | Fixed NFT only; internal parent workflow, never an operator transaction. | `caller: address` — Actual governance/bootstrap caller forwarded by NFT.<br>`originalData: bytes` — Exact original NFT msg.data for updatePublicDrop,updateAllowList,updateDropURI,updateCreatorPayoutAddress,updateAllowedFeeRecipient or updatePayer. | `encoded: bytes` — Encoded same-singleton zero-value typed call; no arbitrary target, value, selector or executor. |
| `restoreMint(uint256,address,uint8)`<br>nonpayable | Reconstruct original-buyer and phase books during ordered V10 NFT import.<br>Rejects missing/zero recipient, duplicate allowlist entitlement or unordered record. Does not issue a new draw, transfer a token or change imported current ownership. | Fixed NFT only while importing state2; not an operator repair/reset endpoint. | `ordinal: uint256` — Next one-based original mint ordinal, exactly totalMinted+1 and within imported NFT count.<br>`recipient: address` — Authenticated ORIGINAL source beneficiary, not the current NFT owner.<br>`phase: uint8` — Authenticated source stage1 team,2 allowlist or3 public. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZV4RouteExecutor

One immutable parent's typed single-hop hookless v4 swap helper. Returns all successful output to that parent; no arbitrary recipient, calldata, rescue or sweep.

[Source](../src/ZZZV4RouteExecutor.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

Native zero asset uses the dedicated sentinel; fee and tick spacing must be valid.<br>`policy.source: address`<br>`policy.output: address`<br>`policy.key: (address,address,uint24,int24,address)` (struct PoolKey)<br>`policy.key.currency0: address`<br>`policy.key.currency1: address`<br>`policy.key.fee: uint24`<br>`policy.key.tickSpacing: int24`<br>`policy.key.hooks: address`<br>`policy.poolManager: address`<br>`policy.stateView: address`<br>`policy.sourceCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`policy.outputCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`policy.poolManagerCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`policy.stateViewCodeHash: bytes32`<br>`policy.reviewHash: bytes32` | `id: bytes32` — Chain/helper/parent/policy-bound route identity. A duplicate is rejected. |
