Parent funds/pulls exact input; prior prefunds cannot subsidize the swap. | Only immutable parent, enabled validated route, nonreentrant. | `id: bytes32` — Recorded enabled route ID.<br>`amountIn: uint256` — Exact raw source units; positive and no greater than int128.max.<br>`minimumOut: uint256` — Positive minimum raw output units, no greater than int128.max; parent also applies USD loss limits.<br>`deadline: uint256` — Unix seconds, from current block time through at most 10 minutes ahead. | `amountOut: uint256` — Actual exact raw output transferred to parent; requires minimumOut and unchanged prefunded balances. |
| `setRouteEnabled(bytes32,bool)`<br>nonpayable | Disable without querying dependencies; re-enable only after complete route identity/dependency/initialized-pool validation. | Only immutable parent; existing route; nonreentrant. | `id: bytes32` — Previously recorded route ID.<br>`enabled: bool` — False stops new swaps; true revalidates before enabling. Existing funds/entitlements are not swept. | None in ABI. |
| `unlockCallback(bytes)`<br>nonpayable | Authenticate and consume the pending payload once, execute the typed PoolManager swap/settlement and verify input/output deltas. | Only the exact temporarily recorded PoolManager with matching pending payload hash; not an operator-callable swap API. | `payload: bytes` — ABI encoding of the already committed PoolKey, source/output, amountIn and minimumOut; arbitrary payloads cannot open a new swap. | `output0: bytes` — ABI-encoded uint256 actual amountOut returned to the active parent execute call. |

## ZZZVault

Native ETH/fixed-ZZZ shared principal and per-NFT economic rights,with scoped mutable operating policy. Entrant-only verified deployment,reviewed mint and terminal direct holder return preserve exact earned rewards/royalties.

[Source](../src/ZZZVault.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,uint256,address)`<br>nonpayable | Bind native canonical chain and reviewed standalone Budget after ProtocolPolicy binding. | Deployment only; nonzero chain,matching Budget controller/capability and prebound policy required. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController with code and bound ProtocolPolicy1.<br>`nftHomeChain: uint256` — Nonzero canonical NFT home chain ID.<br>`budget: address` — Predeployed MigrationBudget matching controller and compound4. Mint recipient belongs to ProtocolPolicy,not this constructor. | None in ABI. |

## ZZZVaultCanonical

Immutable Vault companion for ordinary read-only owner/contribution/holder-exit validation and finite canonical export/import of captured backing. No configurable executor or delegatecall; typed validators cannot move custody.

[Source](../src/ZZZVaultCanonical.sol). Category: Project contract.

### Deployment

| Function / mutability | Purpose | Who / conditions | Inputs | Outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `constructor(address,address,uint256)`<br>nonpayable | Deploy and bind Fixed codec for original Vault/NFT identity, shared shares, pool aliases and classified principal backing. | Deployment only; required creator/controller/parent checks apply before this instance can be bound/sealed. | `controller: address` — Fixed AccessController address for this deployment’s roles and lifecycle.<br>`vaultController: address` — Existing accounting7 Vault with this controller; immutable parent, not a separate custody authority.<br>`chain: uint256` — Original canonical chain ID retained for lineage identity. | None in ABI. |

<a id="maintenance"></a>

## Coverage and maintenance

The machine-readable [reference snapshot](function-reference.json) retains full ABI, canonical signatures, source hashes, reviewed explanations and uncovered explanations. [Explanation inputs](function-reference-notes.json) are the editable source. `python scripts/generate-function-reference.py --check` checks current source bindings and deterministic generated content without compiling or rerunning contract tests.

After a contract change, first obtain source-matching compiler input/output and normal size evidence, update the explanations, then regenerate. Unchanged source needs only the documentation check. A stale ABI, unknown note, duplicate signature, unexpected contract or altered generated file fails that check.

From the repository root, edit `docs/function-reference-notes.json`, then replace these example artifact paths with the reviewed matching files. Use `python3` if that is the Python command in your environment. This command imports existing compiler evidence; it does not compile or rerun contract tests.

```text
python scripts/generate-function-reference.py --compiler-input reviewed/compiler-input.json --compiler-output reviewed/compiler-output.json --size-report reviewed/size-gates.json
python scripts/generate-function-reference.py --check
```

8 current internal-only libraries have no callable ABI and are excluded: `ExactAsset`, `ExactLPAllocation`, `ExactRoyaltyAllocation`, `ExactToken`, `Ids`, `ZZZArtwork`, `ZZZPriceMath`, `ZZZV3VenueValidation`.
